## Document 025: 2014年GPIF改革から考える国内債券の位置づけ ～「金利のある世界」で現行配分は最適か～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2014年GPIF改革から考える国内債券の位置づけ ～「金利のある世界」で現行配分は最適か～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Research Report

2026年 11月 6日

1/7 
2014年 GPIF改革から考える国内債券の位置づけ 
～「金利のある世界」で現行配分は最適か～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  奥田 宏二

（要旨） 
○ GPIFが基本ポートフォリオを変更し、国内債券の割合を引き上げるのではないかとの観測が
市場で浮き沈みしている。本稿では、現在の基本ポートフォリオの起点となった 2014年の運
用改革の成立過程を分析し、変更する場合にどのようなことが起き得るのか考察する。 
○ 2014年改革では、基本ポートフォリオに占める国内債券を60％から 35％へ大幅に引き下げ、
国内外の株式を約 2倍に増やした。アベノミクスの象徴の一つとして GPIF改革は語られる
ことが多いが、改革につながる議論は民主党政権期から始まっていた。 
○ 当時のGPIF運用委員会の議事録によれば、株式をどこまで増やすかより、国内債券をどこま
で減らすかが議論の中心だった。また、当時決定した国内債券 35％という配分は、期待収益
率やリスクだけで機械的に決まったものではなく、移行コストや市場への影響などを考慮し
て決定された。市場への影響を抑えるため、正式決定前から乖離許容幅を使って国内債券を
減らしていたのは、今後の動向を見極めるうえで興味深い。当面、四半期の運用実績に注目
が集まるだろう。 
○ 足元では長期金利が上昇し、低金利が懸念点だった 2014年当時とは逆の環境にある。当時の
運用委員会では、金利が上がれば国内債券の期待収益率が高まり、最適化をやり直すことで
国債の割合が上昇し得るとの説明がなされていた。当時の考え方を踏まえれば、現在の基本
ポートフォリオが「金利のある世界」でも最適なのか再考の余地があろう。

### Page 2

2/7 
1. はじめに 
年金積立金管理運用独立行政法人（GPIF）を巡り、基本ポートフォリオの見直し観測が市場で
浮き沈みしている。きっかけは片山さつき財務・金融担当相が2026年7月、GPIFをはじめとする
年金基金について、日本の金融資産への投資をさらに増やす方向を後押ししたいとの考えを示し
たことだ。長期金利が上昇するなかでの発言で、日本の金融資産とは特に国内債券だと解されて
いる。こうしたなか、GPIF側では同年 8月の経営委員会の議事次第に「基本ポートフォリオ検証
等PTにおける議論について」が報告事項に入り、経営委員会への関心が高まった。 
GPIFの基本ポートフォリオは 5年に 1度、公的年金の財政検証を踏まえて見直す。現在の基本
ポートフォリオは2025年度が開始年だ。制度上、毎年度検証し、必要があれば 5年間の中期目標
期間中でも見直しを検討することになっているものの、GPIF発足後、中期目標期間の途中で基本
ポートフォリオを変更したのは 2013年 6月と 2014年 10月の 2回にとどまる。 
2014年10月の見直しでは、国内債券比率を 60％から 35％へ引き下げ、国内外の株式をそれぞ
れ12％から25％へ引き上げる大幅な変更が行われた。株式比率の上昇によって損失が大きくなる
リスクを懸念する向きはあったが、運用損失だけでなく、年金財政上、目標とする利回りを達成
できないこともリスクと捉える考え方を明確にした。2014年改革の本質は公的年金運用における
「安全」という概念を変えたことだと筆者はみている。 
では、仮にいま国内債券を再び増やすとなれば、改革の逆行を意味するのか。この問いを考え
るため、年金財政からの議論と成長戦略からの要請という二つの流れを民主党政権期からたど
り、2014年改革がどのように実現したのかを振り返る。

2. 民主党政権期――国債中心の「安全運用」への疑問 
2014年改革は第2次安倍晋三政権の経済政策「アベノミクス」の象徴の一つとして語られるこ
とが多い。しかし、その成立過程をさかのぼると、改革につながる議論は第 2次安倍政権の発足
前からあった。民主党政権期の議論では、公的年金の運用における「安全」を巡る議論が次第に
成熟していったことが確認できる。 
2006年に発足したGPIFの基本ポートフォリオは、当初国内債券が 67％を占めていた（資料 1）。
このうち、大半を占める国債は満期に額面で償還される。国債偏重の狙いは、厚生労働省の検討
会が 2010年12月にまとめた報告書にも表れている。公的年金運用の考え方として、保険料は
「投資を目的として徴収されたもの」ではないとして、「安全運用が基本」とした（注 1）。検討会
では、全額を国債で運用すべきだとの意見もあった。

### Page 3

3/7 
資料 1 GPIFの基本ポートフォリオの変遷

（注）2014年 10月以降は短期資産を基本ポートフォリオに含めない。 
（出所）GPIFの公表資料より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

もっとも、国債を多く持つことが本当に安全なのかという疑問はすでにこの検討会で示されて
いた。運用上のリスクを単なる損失だけで捉えず、年金財政上予定された収益を下回り、将来の
保険料率の引き上げや給付の引き下げにつながる可能性まで考慮する必要があるとしている。国
債を多く持てば安全だという単純な整理には収まらない議論が存在していたことが確認できる。 
この検討会に続く社会保障審議会年金部会の専門委員会では、国債への集中はリスクになると
いう考えが強く示されるようになる。2012年 2月に同委員会の議論を整理した資料（注2）では
国債のみの運用は一つの資産への集中であり、分散投資ができていないという意味でリスクが非
常に大きい、という指摘が確認できる。デフレと低金利がこの先も続くとは考えにくく、物価や
賃金が上昇すれば、給付する年金額は増える一方で債券価格は下落し、損失を被る可能性が高い
というアベノミクス下での改革を先取りするような意見も出た。国債のみの運用で、将来にわた
り物価や賃金の上昇率を上回る利回りを確保できるのか疑問が残る、との意見もあった。 
民主党政権後期の議論は、短期の運用損失を過度に重視するのではなく、国債偏重によって年
金財政上、必要な利回りを確保できないリスクを考慮する方向へと発展していった。

3. 産業競争力会議――成長戦略からの見直し要請 
GPIF改革に至る過程では、日本経済の成長戦略の文脈で国債偏重の見直しを求める意見があっ
た。これは安倍政権時だけでなく民主党政権期にもあった。国内債券を減らすべきだという方向
は、年金財政からの議論と重なる。ただ、見直しを求める理由は両政権期とも公的年金の巨額の
資金を経済成長に生かすという発想から来ている。 
安倍政権でGPIF改革の舞台となったのは、政権発足直後の2013年 1月に始まった産業競争力会
議だ。「公的・準公的資金の有効活用」が初回から議論にのぼり、安全資産に偏った年金などの運
用を見直し、成長分野への資金供給につなげる考え方が示された。同会議が進むなかでは、民間

### Page 4

4/7 
議員から「アクティブ運用をし、そこに中長期でもの言う株主ということで企業が新陳代謝も進
めるようなツールとしていく」といった発言が飛び出している（注 3）。 GPIFの資金を企業改革や
経済成長に生かそうとする考え方であり、「専ら被保険者の利益」のために運用するという年金積
立金の原則との緊張をはらんでいた。

4. 有識者会議――成長戦略と被保険者利益の両立 
成長戦略のなかで出たこうした要請を、公的年金の運用原則とどのように両立させたのだろう
か。この問題に対応したのが「公的・準公的資金の運用・リスク管理等の高度化等に関する有識
者会議」だった。この会議は政府の日本再興戦略を受けて経済再生担当相の下に設置され、GPIF
改革の理論的柱を打ち立てた。 
公的年金の運用に国民が何を最も期待しているのか明確に定義した点がこの会議のポイントだ
ろう。2013年 11月の報告書は、「現役世代の保険料負担を維持しつつ、将来の年金給付に支障が
生じないよう、年金財政の長期的な健全性の確保に資すること」こそが国民の最も期待している
ことだとした（注4）。目標とする運用利回りが達成できなければ、保険料の引き上げか将来の年
金給付水準の引き下げが迫られる可能性が生じる。 
この定義に則り、デフレから脱却して適度なインフレ環境へ移るというアベノミクスの目指し
た環境でのポートフォリオのあり方を検討した。安全資産とされる国債を多く持てば安全だと考
えるのではなく、年金財政に必要な収益を確保できるかという観点から基本ポートフォリオを考
える方向が明確に示された。 
収益率の向上という文脈のなかで、産業競争力会議で出たようなGPIFの資金を企業改革に生か
すというアイデアは運用改革に取り込まれていく。投資先企業との対話や議決権行使を通じて企
業価値の向上を促し、その成果を運用収益として享受するという考え方が報告書で示された。成
長戦略が期待した企業改革を、被保険者の利益につながる運用につなげたのだ。 
社会保障審議会年金部会の専門委員会もこれに呼応する形で、デフレから脱却して適度なイン
フレ環境へ移る状況では、あらかじめ「国内債券中心」と示す必要はなく、GPIFが運用目的・目
標に沿ったポートフォリオを検討すべきだとした。年金積立金の運用目標は、年金給付費が長期
的には名目賃金上昇率に連動することから、賃金上昇率をどの程度上回るかで設定される。同専
門委員会は、財政検証に向けて検討した複数の経済前提を踏まえ、GPIFが確保すべき運用利回り
として、名目賃金上昇率を 1.7％上回る水準を示した（注5）。

5. GPIF運用委員会――国内債券 35％の決定過程 
ここから2014年 10月の基本ポートフォリオ変更に至る議論を、当時のGPIF運用委員会の議事
録からたどる。2014年改革は、株式を増やした改革とみられがちだ。しかし、運用委員会の議論
の中心になったのは株式の比率ではなく、国内債券の扱いだった。後年公表された議事録を読む
と、国内債券中心では目標の利回りが達成できないどころか、金利が上昇した場合には多額の損
失を被るといった懸念が、当時の事務局や理事長から示されている。当時の長期金利は 0.5％前後

### Page 5

5/7 
と、歴史的な低水準にあった（資料 2）。事務局の試算も、国内債券の比率を 45％以下に減らさな
ければ目標の利回りに届かない、という形で示された。

資料 2 日本国債 10年物利回り

（注）10年物国債利回りの月末値。2026年 11月は 6日の値。 
（出所）Bloombergより第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

GPIF内部の議論では株式をどこまで増やすかではなく、国内債券をどこまで減らすかが議論の
焦点だったことが分かる。運用委員会の委員長は最終段階で、「国債を減らしたというのが一番の
主で、その結果として、こういうポートフォリオが書けた」と振り返っているのが象徴的だろう
（注 6）。 
基本ポートフォリオの決定に至る議論で興味深いのは、GPIFの売買が国内市場に与える影響を
相当程度考慮に入れていた点だ。事務局から示された基本ポートフォリオ候補は当初、五つあっ
た。このなかには、現在のポートフォリオである 4資産を 25％ずつとする案も含まれていた。こ
の案は、現状からの移行を考えると「現実感がゼロ」とされ、早い段階で検証の対象から外れた
（注 7）。事務局は候補を絞り込む際に足元の資産構成からの距離を物差しに加え、最終案の選定
理由にも「足下の資産構成と相対的に近く、移行に係るコストが小さい」ことを挙げている（注
8）。最終段階では、1％刻みで確認すると、経済中位ケースでは国内債券 34％などのより効率的な
配分も見つかった。それでも、国内債券をさらに減らして国内株式を増やせば移行の影響が大き
くなるとして、35％が維持された。外国市場に比べ、国内市場ではGPIFの売買の影響が大きいと
いう判断が働いていた。 
国内債券の売却は、正式な決定を待たずに始まっていた。運用委員会は2014年4月の時点で、
現行の基本ポートフォリオが認める範囲の中で国内債券を減らす方針を了承している。委員長は

### Page 6

6/7 
その目的を、金利上昇に備えて「債券の評価損を抑える」ことだと確認した（注9）。8月には、
その範囲を超えて国内債券を売り、ほかの資産に振り向ける方針も了承された。事務局は、積立
金規模が巨額であるため、市場インパクトやフロントランニングを誘発せずに速やかに資産配分
を変更することは現実的ではないと説明し、基本ポートフォリオの正式決定前から資産配分の変
更を検討する考えを示した（注 10）。正式決定に先立つ 8月末には、国内債券の比率はすでに
50％程度まで下がっていた。 
GPIFの現在の中期目標でも、市場の価格形成や民間の投資行動等を歪めないことが求められて
いる。今後、基本ポートフォリオを変更する場合にも、市場への影響を抑えるため、正式な変更
に先立って資産配分を調整することが考えられる。今回も運用の動向を見極めるうえで、四半期
の運用実績が注目される。

6. おわりに 
2014年にGPIFが中期目標期間の途中で基本ポートフォリオを変更した背景には、先行きの運用
環境の変化を見通した判断があった。GPIFは当時、日本経済が「長年続いたデフレからの転換と
いう大きな運用環境の変化の節目」にあるとし、「長期的な経済環境の変化に速やかに対応する観
点から、来年度を待たず」に変更に踏み切った（注 11）。実際には低金利がその後も長く続いた
が、足元では当時懸念されていた金利上昇が現実のものとなっている。 
長期金利は2026年 9月に 3％台に達し、約 30年ぶりの水準を付けた。奇しくも3％という数字
は、2014年のGPIF運用委員会でもたびたび登場していた。当時の理事長は、国内債券 60％という
従来の基本ポートフォリオについて、「国内債券の利回りが 3％というものを大前提としてつくっ
ている」と述べていた（注 12）。  
興味深いのは、2014年の運用委員会で、金利が上昇すれば国内債券の将来の期待収益率が高ま
り、改めて資産配分の最適化を行うことで「自然に国債の割合がまたもとに戻るというか、上が
っていく」とGPIFの事務局側が説明していたことだ（注 13）。 2020年の基本ポートフォリオ変更
では、国内金利の低下を踏まえ、国内債券を減らして外国債券を増やした。今後は、長期の収益
確保を担う株式の比率を維持しつつ、為替リスクなどを考慮して外国債券の一部を国内債券に振
り向けることも選択肢として考えられる。2014年に国内債券を減らしたのと同じ考え方が、今度
はその割合を引き上げる判断につながり得るのではないだろうか。

## Document 132: 261110_daiwa_hon-precision-buy-initiation

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Hon Precision, the global leader 
in test handlers and active thermal control (ATC) systems, with a Buy (1) 
rating. According to management, the company commands >70% market 
share in AI/HPC final test (FT) and c.50% in system-level test (SLT) by 
revenue, distinctly positioning it as one of the key global beneficiaries of the 
rising silicon complexity.

Multi-layer content stacking supports ASP and margin growth. As test 
durations continue to lengthen and thermal requirements rise, customers 
are required to deploy greater parallel testing capacity and higher-
specification handlers, creating a structural demand tailwind for both FT 
and SLT handler. We forecast rising AI chip power consumption and 
package complexity over our forecast horizon to drive adoption of higher-
value ATC systems, mega-tray handlers (adding 25-30% to ASP relative to 
standard dual-temp ATC systems), and AOI integration (adding a further 
10-15% to system ASP), supporting a multi-year increase in content per 
tool and expanding its gross margin from 56.8% to 59.2% over 2026-28E.

Recurring consumables and CPO testing expand long-term growth 
visibility. Cold plates generate high-margin, annuity-like revenue with 
every new chip platform which should benefit from shortening AI processor 
refresh cycles. Beyond consumables, Hon Precision is extending its testing 
platform into co-packaged optics (CPO), where optical-electrical co-testing 
could unlock a new addressable market beyond traditional GPU and ASIC 
testing. While near-term contribution remains limited, we view CPO as a 
strategic growth option that enhances the company’s long-term earnings 
visibility and valuation premium over our forecast horizon.

Catalysts: 1) capacity expansion ramp in 2027; 2) mega-tray penetration 
and higher-power ATC adoption; 3) initial CPO test solution qualification.

Valuation: We initiate with a Buy (1) rating and 12M TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied on our 2027E EPS. The stock 
currently trades at c.19x forward PER, despite our projected 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 55%, implying a PEG of only 0.35x. We believe its current 
valuation heavily discounts macro noise with share price pulling back by 
c.53% from its historical high and derating from a cycle-peak multiple of 45-
50x 1-year-forward PER. Backed by solid order visibility through late-2027, 
we highlight Hon Precision’s risk-reward profile as decisively asymmetric.

Risks: 1) Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment; 2) delayed ramp-up of 
new products; and 3) potential loss of market share.

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Hon Precision is the global leader in semiconductor test handlers and active thermal 
control (ATC) systems. The company dominates the global AI/HPC final test handler market, supported 
by its advanced thermal management technologies. With over 70% market share in AI/HPC test 
handling, Hon Precision benefits from growing demand for AI, HPC and custom ASIC applications. Its 
solutions are widely adopted by leading OSATs and foundries worldwide, making it a key enabler of 
advanced semiconductor testing.

### Page 6

6

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Growth Catalysts 
Multi-factor dollar content stacking 
We believe the primary growth catalyst for Hon Precision over our forecast horizon stems 
from multi-layered dollar content stacking, where the rise in machine unit value should 
outpace volume growth. Next-gen AI accelerators have historically exhibited more than 50-
70% increase in thermal design power (TDP) per generation, with platforms like Nvidia 
Rubin and Rubin Ultra surging to 2.3kW and 3.6kW vs. Blackwell’s 1.2kW to 1.4kW. 
Managing these extreme thermal loads makes high-end active thermal control (ATC) a 
necessity, driving a structural step-up in ASPs from standard handlers at approximately 
TWD6m to dual-temperature ATC systems at TWD12-15m, and tri-temperature ATC 
systems at TWD15-18m.

Concurrently, as the package size of next-generation ASICs/GPUs increases from the 
current 120mm tray specification to around 150mm, Hon Precision is introducing mega-
tray large-package solutions in October 2026, which command a 25-30% ASP premium 
over mainstream GPU handlers. As the majority of next-generation ASIC/GPU is set to 
exceed the boundary of 120mm, we estimate shipment penetration for mega-tray solutions 
will likely reach 40% in 2027E. Integrated functions of Automated Optical Inspection (AOI) 
will contribute an additional 10-15% ASP uplift to unit value of handler. Furthermore, this 
content expansion extends into the flagship mobile AP market as smartphone processors 
transition to multi-die packaging. For example, Apple’s A20 Pro processor has already 
adopted wafer-level multi-chip module (WMCM) packaging in 2026, requiring high-end ATC 
systems capable of managing up to 3,000W thermal dissipation to resolve device self-
heating.

Hon Precision: ASP multipliers

Source: Daiwa research

High-margin cold plate consumables  
In addition to capital equipment set sales, Hon Precision’s cold plate consumables 
business serves as a crucial recurring revenue engine and a primary driver of gross margin 
expansion. Cold plates carry the highest gross margin across all company product lines. 
Driven by the compression of AI processor lifecycles to 1-1.5 years (down from 2-3 years 
historically), each test handler requires 5-10 distinct, custom-engineered cold plates 
tailored to specific die footprints, socket layouts, and thermal hot-spot geometries. To 
conduct parallel test and maintain the throughput of chip testing, test handler for system 
level test (SLT) requires even more cold plates, which can go up to 24 plates. As a result, 
cold plate tooling generates high-frequency annuity-like sales that recur with every new 
chip platform iteration, independent of major equipment replacements.

Hon Precision: cold plate revenue trend

Source: Company, Daiwa research

Testing duration and volume multiples 
We believe the rapid rise in AI chip complexity is driving structural growth in semiconductor 
testing intensity, positioning Hon Precision as a key global beneficiary through its 
leadership in high-parallelism test handlers. Driven by the mainstream adoption of 
heterogeneous integration (such as TSMC’s CoWoS and Intel’s EMIB), verifying complex 
AI silicon has extended test durations by 2x to 10x vs. standard consumer chips (5 to 10 
seconds). Final test durations for AI GPUs have expanded from hundreds of seconds to 
over 10 minutes per chip. To maintain daily manufacturing throughput despite this 10x 
spike, chipmakers must scale up massive parallel testing architectures and procure 
significantly more handlers, cementing Hon Precision as the primary test handler partner 
for tier-one US AI GPU and CPU players.

Concurrently, escalating chip complexity, TDP, and die costs make field return (RMA) 
penalties far exceed testing expenses, forcing cloud service providers (CSPs) and 
automotive OEMs to mandate system-level testing (SLT). Because SLT executes over 
extended durations ranging from minutes to hours per chip, high-volume production 
mandates automated parallel SLT handlers equipped with high-capacity ATC. Holding 
c.50% market share in AI/HPC SLT handlers alongside Chroma, Hon Precision is 
expanding beyond GPUs into custom CSP ASICs (eg, Google TPU and AWS) and server 
CPUs. As SLT adoption broadens across non-GPU devices, Hon Precision leverages this 
multi-year runway to transition into a full-spectrum semiconductor testing platform.

Global: growing chip complexity – testing time by AI/HPC chip

Source: Daiwa research

CPO unlocks new TAM beyond GPUs 
Beyond conventional electrical testing, Co-Packaged Optics (CPO) represents a multi-year 
paradigm shift towards a multimodal optical-electrical testing platform. Hon Precision 
currently monetises initial CPO deployments by supplying Final Test (FT) handlers for pure 
electrical testing (E/E test) of switch ASICs and Optical Engines (OEs) on production 
platforms such as Nvidia’s Spectrum-X and Quantum-X.

### Page 8

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

However, the strategic growth catalyst is anchored in next-generation Insertion 4 (package-
level optical and electrical co-testing, O/E co-test). Because an undetected defect in a 
single optical component would force manufacturers to scrap an entire high-value CPO 
module, early and rigorous package-level validation is imperative for yield protection. 
Collaborating directly with ATE leader Teradyne and optical instrument specialists (such as 
Keysight), Hon Precision is developing a specialised ATC handler capable of simultaneous 
optical and electrical validation, including Bit Error Rate (BER), optical loopback, crosstalk, 
and high-power thermal management testing for integrated ASIC and Optical Engine 
modules in a single touchdown.

Engaged with Nvidia CPO switch programmes and actively expanding to additional ASIC 
clients, Hon Precision targets engineering verification in late-2026 and initial shipments in 
2027, ahead of 2028 volume production. Although we expect revenue contribution will 
likely be relatively low (<5% in 2027-2028), it drives valuation and market expectation to 
serve larger total addressable market (TAM).

Market Analysis 
Global testing equipment market 
According to TechInsights, global testing equipment capex is projected to expand 
significantly from USD8.3bn in 2024 to USD26.1bn by 2027E, representing a 46.4% 
CAGR. Notably, while SoC test systems front-loaded strong growth in 2024-25, before 
tapering to 9.6% YoY in 2027E on TechInsights estimates, package handling equipment is 
forecasted to exhibit strong structural resilience and longevity. Based on TechInsights, 
market capex for package handling equipment will likely more than double from USD941m 
in 2024 to USD2.6bn by 2027E (40.9% CAGR), re-accelerating to 46% YoY growth in 
2027E from 34% YoY in 2025. This cadence reflects the phased deployment of test 
infrastructure – initial tester investments are followed by high-volume back-end handling 
installations as advanced packaging (eg, CoWoS, Chiplets) scales into mass production.

Global semi testing equipment: capex  Global semi testing equipment: handler and prober growth

Source: TechInsights, Daiwa research  Source: TechInsights, Daiwa research

Handler market players 
Hon Precision’s overall share in the global test handler TAM rose from 35% in 2024 to 57% 
in 2025, and we forecast this to reach 75% in 2026E and 79% by 2027E. Within the high-
value AI and HPC Final Test (FT) handler segment, Hon Precision maintains a dominant 
market share, exceeding 70% in both sales value and shipment volume, per management. 
In the rapidly expanding AI and HPC SLT handler segment, Hon Precision holds c.50% 
market share in terms of revenue, per management, alongside its primary peer, Chroma, 
establishing a duopoly in global high-end SLT testing.

Hon Precision: handler market share forecasts

Source: Daiwa research

Outside of AI and HPC logic testing, the broader test handler landscape comprises global 
and regional vendors, including Cohu (COHU US, NR), Advantest (6857 JP, JPY28,375, 
Outperform [2]; covered by Daiwa analyst Takeshi Fukuyama), and mainland Chinese 
suppliers such as Changchuan Technology (300604 CH, NR) and JHT Design (603061 
CH, NR).

### Page 10

10

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

While global incumbents rely on brand prestige, standard architectures, and bundled 
offerings, Hon Precision excels in leading-edge AI/HPC testing through its ultra-high-power 
Active Thermal Control (ATC) and rapid engineering turnaround. As multi-kilowatt dynamic 
loads (1.5kW–3kW+) and extreme warpage on >120mm packages push standard handlers 
beyond their limits, Hon Precision’s hyper-customised ATC fluidics, high-force micro-
mechanics, and modular designs deliver superior millisecond thermal clamping and agile 
package adaptation. Furthermore, while giants like Advantest dominate the SoC ATE tester 
market (>55% share), their in-house handlers focus primarily on memory and standard 
logic. Consequently, tier-one chipmakers and OSATs standardly decouple tester and 
handler sourcing, thus pairing Advantest or Teradyne testers with Hon Precision’s high-
power ATC handlers. Located directly within Taiwan’s advanced packaging cluster, Hon 
Precision provides real-time, on-site co-validation and custom cold-plate engineering that 
preserve formidable entry barriers against bundled solutions.

Meanwhile, although Chinese competitors have expanded aggressively in domestic mature 
nodes and conventional Pick-and-Place handlers, Hon Precision commands a decisive 
generational lead in high-complexity advanced packaging applications. Chinese vendors 
remain largely confined to lower-wattage consumer, automotive, and industrial IC testing, 
lacking the proven patents, structural rigidity, and micro-level force balancing required to 
safely apply hundreds of kilograms of contact force onto fragile, multi-chip CoWoS 
packages without cracking dies or warping substrates. Moreover, because enterprise AI 
chips cost tens of thousands of dollars each, tier-1 global fabless leaders and North 
American cloud service providers (CSPs) maintain virtually zero tolerance for testing 
failure, establishing an entrenched validation moat for Hon Precision. Free from 
geopolitical export constraints, Hon Precision can seamlessly deploy across global OSAT 
networks in Taiwan, North America, and Southeast Asia.

Balance sheet and cash flow 
Driven by surging AI/HPC testing demand, Hon Precision is actively expanding its capacity 
across the new Desheng facility, localised production at the Changshu site, and 
debottlenecking of headquarter efficiency. The company targets 40-50% capacity growth in 
2026 and another 50% increase in 2027. The expansion of manufacturing space and 
production facilities requires an increase in capital expenditure, which we project Hon 
Precision will ramp up to approximately TWD2.8bn in 2026E, TWD3.8bn in 2027E, and 
TWD4.6bn in 2028E. The company’s operating cash inflows are more than sufficient to 
fully fund factory expansions alongside a c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Hon Precision continues to maintain an un-levered balance sheet with zero financial bank 
debt. Cash and cash equivalents exceeded TWD53bn in 2025; we project this figure to 
surpass TWD75bn by 2028E. Its robust balance sheet underpins peer-leading shareholder 
returns, with ROE to expand from 34.5% in 2025 to 59.6% by 2028E. This self-funding 
cash engine supports management’s targeted c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Valuation and recommendation 
Initiating coverage with a Buy (1) rating 
We initiate coverage of Hon Precision with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied to our 2027E EPS forecast. When the stock rallied 
above TWD8,000 in 1H26, its forward PER multiple stretched to a cycle peak of 45-50x. 
However, we expect key growth catalysts, including a 50% aggregate capacity expansion, 
alongside higher-ASP shipments of large-form-factor mega tray handlers and co-packaged 
optics (CPO) test solutions, to drive its top line, primarily in 2H27. With existing production 
lines operating at full utilisation, investors are concerned over a potential temporary 
deceleration in growth momentum through 2H26-1H27, ahead of the next potential major 
capacity inflection in 2H27.

However, as the calendar rolls into 2027, we believe market attention will swiftly refocus on 
the company’s multi-year structural thesis. Easing capacity bottlenecks, combined with 
robust ASIC-driven adoption of mega tray architectures and ramping CPO test platforms, 
should spark a powerful volume-and-ASP expansion cycle. The stock is currently trading at 
a 19x 1-year-forward PER vs. its earnings CAGR of 55% over 2026-28E, translating into a 
0.35x PEG ratio. We believe the valuation now heavily discounts the near-term pause in 
revenue and macro noises, presenting a compelling risk-reward profile for long-term 
investors.

Risks to our call 
1. Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment: A slowdown in AI infrastructure spending 
or delays in GPU, ASIC and server CPU ramps could reduce demand for high-end 
FT/SLT handlers and thermal-control solutions, weakening order growth and revenue 
visibility. 
2. Slower-than-expected new product adoption: Customer qualification delays or 
slower adoption of mega-tray handlers, AOI inspection systems and CPO FT solutions 
could delay product ramps, resulting in revenue growth below our expectations. 
3. Potential share loss: Competitors’ advances in thermal control, customer qualification 
of alternative suppliers or delays in meeting new testing requirements could weaken 
Hon Precision’s position on next-generation platforms, affecting market share and 
pricing power.

### Page 15

15

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
Company introduction 
Hon Precision was founded in Taichung in 2015, building on the technology and industry 
experience of Hon Technology, which was established in 1999. The company develops 
semiconductor test handlers and thermal-control solutions for AI GPUs, AI ASICs, server 
CPUs, automotive chips and consumer electronics, supporting customers from new-
product introduction (NPI) through volume production.

Hon Precision has two main product categories:

• Semiconductor test handlers: Final-test (FT) and system-level-test (SLT) handlers 
automate chip loading, positioning and sorting. These platforms can incorporate active 
thermal control (ATC) systems to maintain chips at specified temperatures during 
testing. 
• Testing fixtures and modules: Mainly customised cold plates and socket layout kits 
(SLKs), which provide thermal management and mechanical interfaces for different chip 
packages. Customers may require replacement cold plates and kits as package designs 
change, even when retaining existing handlers.

Hon Precision’s order mix is significantly weighted towards AI/HPC/ASIC applications, 
which accounted for 78% of orders in 1Q-3Q26, up from 72% in 2025. These applications 
include AI computing, servers and networking. Automotive represented 9% of orders, 
followed by mobile AP and communications at 7%, consumer electronics at 4%, and 
memory and MEMS at 2%.

As for capacity, Hon Precision’s manufacturing footprint is primarily based in Taiwan, with 
Taichung serving as the group’s main R&D, engineering, quality management, and 
equipment production hub. In addition, the company operates a Suzhou facility focused on 
manufacturing cold plates and related kits, while Taichung Plant 4 is expected to provide 
incremental production capacity to support future growth.

Shareholder structure 
Hon Precision’s major shareholders include several investment and development 
companies. As of 17 April 2026, Hung Min Development was the largest shareholder with a 
17.37% stake, followed by Hongji Investment at 12.08% and Hong Yu Development at 
7.80%. Chairman Wen-Ta Hsieh also directly held 1.77%. International institutional 
shareholders included Norges Bank and GIC, with disclosed stakes of 1.30% and 0.97%, 
respectively. The company has a free float of 48%, allowing broad public-market 
participation and supporting trading liquidity.

### Page 17

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
What is handler? 
A semiconductor test handler automates the movement, positioning and temperature 
conditioning of packaged chips during testing. It places each device into a test socket, 
applies the required contact force and sorts the device according to the test results. The 
tester performs the electrical measurements, while the handler ensures consistent 
mechanical and thermal conditions.

The process begins with packaged chips loaded into the handler in trays. After temperature 
conditioning, the handler transfers each chip into a test socket connected to the tester 
through a load board. Once electrical contact is established, the tester applies signals and 
measures the chip’s response. For ATC-equipped systems, a customised cold plate works 
with the active thermal control system to maintain the required chip temperature 
throughout testing. The handler then unloads and sorts the chips based on their test 
results.

Semiconductor test handler: workflow and thermal control system

Source: Hon Precision, Daiwa research

Handlers are used in both final test (FT) and system-level test (SLT). FT handlers work 
with automated test equipment (ATE) to check electrical characteristics and functionality 
against specifications. SLT handlers place chips into test positions connected to system-
level platforms, typically comprising test boards, test PCs and power supplies, to evaluate 
operation under conditions resembling actual use.

### Page 18

18

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Semiconductor back-end testing FT workflow

Source: Semiconductor engineering

Thermal control becomes increasingly important as AI/HPC chips consume more power 
and adopt larger, more complex packages. Different test patterns change power 
consumption and heat distribution, requiring dynamic heating or cooling to keep the chip 
within a specified temperature range. Precise handling and temperature control support 
repeatable measurements, while parallel testing and efficient chip movement help improve 
overall test throughput.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Quanta Computer Inc.  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 OpM miss; stay OW
Management said AI demand has remained unchanged in recent
weeks. It reiterates confidence in strong double-digit y/y growth
into 2027. General server demand continues to exceed
expectations YTD, driven by agentic AI.
Platform transition weighs on 4Q shipments; VR200 to support CY27 growth: AI
servers contributed 75–80% of 3Q26 server revenue, with CY26 AI revenue on track
for triple-digit y/y growth and ~65% of Quanta’s total revenue. The platform

transition is expected to drive a q/q decline in 4Q26 NVL72 rack shipments, but
management’s tone suggests a milder decline than our prior forecast of 4K racks (vs.
~6.2K in 3Q26). We therefore raise our 4Q26 NVL72 production forecast 1K, to 5K,
implying 21.9K builds in CY26. Management expects shipments to accelerate in 1Q27
as VR200 ramps, supporting high double-digit AI server revenue growth in CY27.
ASIC servers’ revenue contribution is projected to double, from high single digits in

CY26 to double digits in CY27.
Agentic AI supports general server growth: Management noted that YTD general
server shipments have exceeded expectations, with both shipments and revenue
increasing q/q in 3Q26 and further growth expected in 4Q26, primarily driven by
agentic AI. Management expects steady double-digit y/y growth in both shipments
and revenue in CY27, with deployments broadly balanced between x86 and Arm-
based platforms and comparable margin profiles. While industry-wide component

shortages persist, particularly in memory, CPUs and PCBs, management indicated
that tier-1 CSPs continue to secure sufficient supply.
Lower NB volumes and rising costs pressure margins: Quanta’s NB shipments fell
17% q/q, to 9.6M units in 3Q26, weaker than normal seasonality but above guidance
of a 20% decline. Rising memory costs and lower scale weighed on profitability, and
we expect margin pressure to persist in 4Q26. Management guides for another

double-digit q/q shipment decline in 4Q26, albeit narrower than in 3Q26, implying a
15–20% y/y decline for CY26. We project 4Q26 shipments of 8.3M units (-14% q/q)
and CY26 shipments of 39.4M units (-15% y/y). For CY27, management expects NB
shipments to decline in high single digits to 10% y/y and PC/NB revenue to fall to a
single-digit share of the mix, reflecting AI server racks’ substantially higher ASPs.
Keep price target at NT$385 and stay OW: Our target implies 11.8x CY27e P/E or

11.0x CY28e P/E, vs. its three-year range of 6-18x. Risk-reward looks attractive at  9.1x
CY27e P/E, below the three-year average of 11.7x, with ~5.1% dividend yield (DPS of
NT$15.6).

### Page 4

M
Update
4
3Q26 Results Summary
Component cost pressure drove 3Q GM miss:
Revenue was NT$1,115.5bn (+8% q/q, +125% y/y), but GM deteriorated to 4.8% (-30bps q/
q, -210bps y/y), which is 40bps below our estimate and 20bps below the Street.
Management cited the  following reasons for the GM miss:
• PC/NB: cost pressures from inflationary component pricing; diminishing economies
of scale.
• Servers: inflationary component pricing pressured server gross margins, despite a

slight decline in AI server shipments.
However, opex was also higher than expected, mainly owing to higher R&D expenses.
Thus, OP came in at NT$32.2bn (-6% q/q, +75% y/y), 7%  below our estimate and 112%
below the Street.
OpM was 2.9% (-40bps q/q, -80bps y/y). Management guided for 2H OpM to be flat h/h,
so we think 3Q OpM was a miss.
Non-op income was higher than expected because of ~NT$3.9bn in FX gains, so net profit
came in at ~NT$28.4bn (-1% q/q, +73% y/y) for EPS of NT$7.36, 6% above our estimate

and 2% above the Street.
Consignment? Under the buy-and-sell model, high-ASP server rack procurement inflated
revenue but diluted gross margins and to a lesser extent operating margins (owing to
operating leverage) via pass-through component accounting. Management continues to
stress absolute profit expansion and sustained >20% ROIC over margin ratios. To mitigate
balance sheet inventory carrying risk and working capital burden, Quanta will continue to

negotiate for a consignment business model with its CSP customers into CY27.

Estimate Changes
We update our model to factor in the 3Q26 margin miss and updated guidance from
management, which leads to increases of 7%, 13%, and 13% changes to our EPS estimates
for 2026, 2027, and 2028, respectively. We primarily raise our revenue assumptions while
lowering our margin assumptions for 2026-2028.

### Page 6

M
Update
6
Price Target and Valuation Methodology
We keep our price target at NT$385, reflecting the offset between raised 2026-28
earnings estimates and a lower medium-term growth rate: This implies 11.8x CY27e and
11.0x CY28e EPS, in line with the average of Quanta's three-year historical range of 11.7x.
We view this as reasonable because we expect the company to benefit from rising AI
server and EV demand over the long term, which will offset the weakness in the PC

segment and drive mix improvements.
We continue to apply a residual income model to derive our price target. Our key
assumptions include:
• A cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of 1.2, equity premium of 6.0%, and risk-free rate of
1.5%)
• A 7.0% medium-term growth rate – lowered from 8.5% previously, as we bake in
some conservatism on concerns over a potential slowdown in data center build-
out following the US midterm elections (see our Global Team's view), which could

affect Quanta's server business directly.
• A 3% terminal growth rate
Our bull and bear case scenario values also stay unchanged at $545 and NT$230,
respectively.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 176: 261112_ml_kingslide

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_ml_kingslide.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

King Slide

Buy: strong 3Q26 results, 88.2% GM; solid 
’27/28E sales/margin; cut PO to NT$12,988

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 12,988 TWD | Price: 8,165 TWD

3Q26 results: another impressive beat with 88.2% GM 
King Slide’s 3Q26 GM/OPM hit record highs of 88.2%/82.9% for the fourth/second 
consecutive quarter, respectively, well above BofAe of 83.7%/78.2% and consensus of 
84.0%/78.6%. OP (+62% QoQ, +383% YoY) was 7%/5% above BofAe/consensus. We 
attribute the solid margins/results to sizable sales scale (+60% QoQ, +297% YoY) and 
better operating efficiency. With non-operating gain (mainly net interest income), NP 
(+65% QoQ, +266% YoY) was 8%/7% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3). 
BofAe 4Q26 sales -20% QoQ; solid 2027/28 sales/margin 
We now expect King Slide’s 4Q26 sales to decline 20% QoQ, from the high 3Q26 base 
and amid GPU platform transition. We believe Sep/Oct sales (-31%/+2% MoM) shall 
serve as evidence for slower order momentum. Our 4Q26 sales forecast implies flat-to-
slightly higher MoM sales in Nov/Dec. We now expect another 45%/20% YoY sales 
growth in 2027/28, with stronger pull-in demand resuming in 1Q27. In our view, 
2027/28 sales should be supported by various AI projects across platforms (GPU/ASIC/ 
AMD) and clients (global/regional CSPs, neoclouds, AI start-ups, enterprises). We also 
keep monitoring the capacity ramp-up from the new US Houston site, which may bring 
further sales upside. We expect high and sustainable GM at 85.0% both in 2027/28. We 
see FX as a potential swing factor to GM, given the company’s natural-hedging policy.  
Trim PO to NT$12,988: lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy 
We lower our 2026-27E EPS by 0-4% but lift our 2028E EPS by 7%, mainly to reflect 
3Q26 beat and stronger margins. Yet, we cut PO to NT$12,988 (28x 2027E EPS) from 
NT$14,888 (32x 2027E EPS), with valuation base unchanged but a lower P/E multiple. 
We now use 28x (2SD above average since IPO) on valuation de-rating across AI supply 
chain amid macro uncertainty. Nevertheless, we reiterate Buy on King Slide in view of its 
lion share in AI server rail market, diversified client/project base, strong margin profile.

Key Metrics

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
King Slide is the global top server rail kit manufacturer from 
Taiwan. It also supplies slide rail kit for kitchen/furniture. Its 
key server clients include global cloud service providers and 
enterprise servers.

King Slide was founded in 1986 and was publicly listed on 
Taiwan Stock Exchange on 25 June 2008. Its headquarter 
and main production sites are located in Kaohsiung 
(Taiwan). It also has capacity in Houston (US).

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to King Slide given its leading 
position in server rail to ride on strong AI market demand 
with rising content value thanks to increasing component 
density and customization. We highlight King Slide's early 
step into server market since 2001 with proven track 
record, much larger scale, time-to-market capability, higher 
automation level, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for 
high/above-peer margins.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
King Slide (KGSLF) 
Our PO of NT$12,988 is based on 28x 2027E EPS. We use 28x P/E multiple (2SD above 
P/E average since listing) in view of strong AI server market demand, leading position in 
server rail, solid/high margin profiles, and double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E. Its 
early step into server market since 2001 with proven track record, much larger scale 
than peer, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for high/above-peer margins.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) even stronger AI server demand, (2) margin upside thanks 
to larger scale and/or better efficiency, (3) better kitchen slides market demand, (4) 
faster capacity expansion, (5) favorable FX movement (mainly USD/NTD) for GM and/or 
non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/components 
shortage, (2) GM pressure due to higher raw material cost (e.g. steel, aluminum), (3) 
softer kitchen slides market demand under macro uncertainty/weakness, (4) delayed 
product certification, (5) slower capacity expansion, (6) unfavorable FX movement 
(mainly USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

## Document 242: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클SK하이닉스(000660) 높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리_0a1416f9

### Page 1

2026년 10월 29일 
기업분석

SK하이닉스 (000660) 
높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클

Buy(유지) 
목표주가 3,150,000원, 현재 주가(10/28): 1,487,000원 
▶ Analyst 박준영 jyp94@hanwha.com 3772-7481 / RA 김나우 now.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7710

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(LTA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 LTA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 LTA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 LTA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

이 자료는 조사분석 담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성됐음을 확인합니다. 
본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 자료를 
기관투자자 또는 제3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (박준영, 김나우) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

5 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

주가 추이

3Q 큰 폭의 판매 감소, 매출/영업이익 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 44.6조원(-4.5% 이하 YoY)과 2.4조원
(-5.4%) 기록하며 시장 기대치 하회 전망. 3Q 매출액은 글로벌 판매 부진 지속
과 환율 하락, 그리고 친환경차 판매 성장 둔화 등의 영향에 기인. 특히, 3Q 판
매량은 918.7만대(-11.5% 이하 YoY)로 전년 동기 대비 큰 폭 감소하며 매출
액에 부정적 영향. 이는 7월과 8월 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생
산 물량 차질이 발생한 부분에 기인. 파업은 기존 물량/믹스 개선에 기여했던 
글로벌 HEV 판매도 둔화(+3.4%)시키면서 매출에 부정적 영향을 심화. 
3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입에 따른 원가 상승 영향과 친환경차 
판매 감소에 따른 부정적 믹스 영향 불가피. 이에 시장 기대치인 3.0조원을 크
게 하회할 전망. 다만, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮아지면서 판매보증비 
외화환산손익 영향은 다소 개선(0.4%p) 예상되며, 관세 영향 또한 전년 동기 
대비 약 1조원(2.1%p) 감소하면서 수익성 악화 영향을 일부 만회할 전망.  
’27년 지연된 신차효과 가시화로 반등 여력 충분 
4Q부터는 3Q 파업 영향 등으로 지연된 신차 확대 본격화되고, 현지 수요 높은 
차종 중심의 물량 확대를 통해 매출 회복 및 이익 성장 가시화가 기대. 아반떼
와 투싼 FMC 확대로 국내 및 해외 판매 둔화가 점진적 만회되고, 지난 9월부
터 터키에서 아이오닉3 생산이 본격화되면서 유럽에서의 시장 부진 회복이 기
대. 또한, GV80과 G80 HEV 모델 출시로 내년부터 국내/북미 중심 판매 확대
되며 올해 부진했던 물량/믹스 개선에 기여할 것으로 기대. ’27년 HMGMA에
서 HEV 신차 생산 투입 예정되어 있어 상대적으로 높은 수요가 지속되는 북미
에서의 HEV 현지 생산 확대로 가동률 증가와 관세 부담 완화도 수익성 개선에 
기여할 것으로 기대. 
부정적 환율 고려 필요하나 ’27년 강한 증익 기대  
’27년 동사 영업이익 추정치는 12.4조원(+22.3%)으로 ’26년 감익에서 ’27년 
증익 전환 예상. 최근 환율 추세 고려 시 내년 상반기까지 부정적인 환율 영향 
확대 가능성 존재하나, 기존 노후 차종에서 현지 수요 대응 가능한 신차 중심 
생산/판매 확대로 물량/믹스 개선되며 부정적 환율 영향 상쇄 기대.  
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-11.8%)과 상반기 적용한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)에 따라 목표주가를 670,000원으로 하향. 그러나, 연 내 RMAC 운영 
성과와 RA 설립, 12월 모셔널 자율주행 상용화와 웨이모向 로보택시 
양산/공급 등 미래 사업에서의 가시성도 여전히 기대 가능한 부분.

이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

1 October 2026
Key points
• The Cotality home value index – covering
all of Australia – fell 1.2% in October
following a 1.3% decline in September
and 1.4% falls in August and July.

Annual growth has turned negative,
running at –2.8%yr with prices down
6.7% from their peak in March.
• Across the major cities, Brisbane and
Perth led the declines in the October
month, recording falls of 2.0% and
1.7% respectively, but the correction
remains more pronounced in Sydney
(prices down a further 1.6%mth to be
off 9.4%yr) with Melbourne also off
more sharply over the year (–7.6%yr)
but recording a milder October
(–0.8%mth). Adelaide remains relatively

resilient, recording a 1.2% decline in
the month but prices still up 3.7%yr.
• Preliminary estimates show the
number of sales, in rolling 3mth
terms, down 5.6%qtr and 22.5%yr.

### Page 2

forecasts are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially
from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The Cotality home value index, covering all of Australia, fell
1.2% in October with slight revisions to previous months
again shaving a further 0.3% off prices. The correction
has now seen prices decline 6.7% from their March peak

with annual growth firmly in negative at –2.8%yr. Turnover
continues fall as well, down 22.5%yr. Housing-related
tax policy changes, interest rate rises and heightened
uncertainty have triggered a sharp contraction in markets.
The downturn is now comparable to the previous two
housing market corrections. Nationally, the downturn in
2022-23 saw prices decline 7.5% and turnover fall 29% peak
to trough. The 2017-18 correction saw prices fall 8.6% and

turnover by 24%, with larger falls in Sydney and Melbourne.
Note that unless specified, figures in titles, tables and
commentary are in non‑seasonally adjusted terms, consistent
with the ‘headline’ figures reported by Cotality. However,
figures shown in charts are in seasonally adjusted terms.
Preliminary estimates of turnover show rolling 3mth volumes
down 5.6%qtr in October, previous estimates again revised
lower. The monthly pattern suggests declines re‑accelerated

through September‑October after stabilising somewhat in July‑
August. The RBA's interest rate rise in late September and a
further expected tightening likely contributed to the shift.
The detail shows Brisbane and Perth are now leading the
way in terms of monthly price declines although corrections
to date remain significantly larger in Sydney and Melbourne
while smaller capitals and regional areas continue to see much
milder downturns. Across price tiers, all segments are now

showing similar rates of decline after top price tiers had seen
a more pronounced and earlier downturn. 'Top tier' segments
have tended to lead recent price cycles.
Looking across jurisdictions,
• Sydney prices dropped 1.6% in October having averaged
similar‑sized declines over the previous five months. While
all segments and tiers continue to record falls, the last few
months has seen the pace of decline moderate a touch in the
top tier segments that have led cycles in the past, although

they are still a long way from stabilisation.
• Melbourne prices declined 0.8%mth, in line with
September's move and continuing the slower pace of
retreat. As noted, this likely reflects the weaker starting
point coming into the correction, prices already at 5½ year
lows. Interestingly, the detail shows the moderation in price
declines in recent months has been more pronounced for
'top tier' units where prices are back where they were in
2016.
Housing downturn deepens

3WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
• Brisbane prices are tracking a very fast rate of decline
that is also seeing large downward revisions. Prices fall
2% in October with September and August now both
showing 2.1% falls, revised down from 1.5% and 1.6%

respectively (August having previously been estimated at
just 1%, July's move having seen a similar series of revisions
–1.2% to –1.8%). The combined effect has taken the rolling
3mth change to –6.1%qtr from –4.7%qtr last months and
–2.7%qtr in August and –0.6%qtr in July. At 0.4%yr, annual
price growth has virtually stalled flat having run at 18%yr as
recently as May. All tiers and segments are recording similar
rates of decline.
• Perth is seeing something very similar, prices dropping a

further 1.7% in October, with September's decline revised
down to 2.1% from –1.2% and August's to 2.0% from –1.7%.
At 3.9%yr, annual growth is still positive but off steeply
from the 24% pace back in May. As with Brisbane, all tiers
and segments are recording similar rates of decline.
• Adelaide dwelling prices posted a somewhat milder 1.2%
decline in October, , September's –1.3% decline marked
down to –1.7%. Annual price growth slowed to 3.7%yr,

similar to Perth's but with less of a whiplash from strong
gains to large declines. Turnover has also been much
steadier, sales holding flat over the last three months and
down just 12.4%yr.
• The smaller capitals were again more mixed, price growth
still positive in Darwin (0.6%mth, 9.7%yr), relatively resilient
in Hobart (+0.5%mth, 5.5%yr) but weaker in Canberra
(–0.7%mth, –3.7%yr).
• Regional markets showed a 0.9% decline in Qld but slightly
milder declines in NSW (–0.7%) and Victoria (0.6%). Annual

prices growth is in the 1.4‑3.5%yr range.
All up, the October updates make for bleak reading. With the
RBA widely expected to raise interest rates again, the near
term outlook remains very challenging. Next week's consumer
sentiment update will show how this is shaping housing‑
related sentiment – so far, consumer price expectations have
weakened but stopped short of swinging to the clear negative
consensus that emerged in the 2022‑23 correction. That now

looks to be at risk. Either way, momentum looks set to remain
weak into year‑end and, barring a significantly improved
inflation and interest rate outlook, well into 2027.

## Document 313: Blue Wave_ Democrats sweep the US midterms

Source PDF: all report pdf/Blue Wave_ Democrats sweep the US midterms.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Blue Wave: Democrats 
sweep the US midterms  
Summary 
Event: The Democrats gained control of the House of 
Representatives and the Senate at the US midterm elections 
that took place this week.  
Our View: With the US government now no longer under 
unified control of the Republicans, gridlock has returned to 
Washington. We believe investors should not overreact to 
these results and should stay invested. 
In equities, investors should continue to diversify their 
equity exposure into more defensive sectors. As we 
transition to mid-cycle for the equity markets, we expect 
stocks to continue to deliver positive returns in the 8-10% 
range per annum, a downshift from the torrid pace of gains in 
the earlier part of the cycle.  
In bonds, income remains attractive given recent spread 
widening. We favour non-hyperscaler investment grade 
corporate bonds at the three to seven-year part of the 
curve. We continue to expect the US dollar to remain range-
bound in the near term, as we expect two more rate hikes 
from the Fed, but as this mini rate hike cycle ends in the 
second quarter of 2027, the USD index (DXY) is likely to 
depreciate towards 98.5 as markets start to anticipate a Fed 
rate cut in the second half of next year.  
Oil is likely to rebound in the short-term on heightened 
geopolitical risk, but as supply chains adapt to the current 
situation and Congress exercises oversight on defense 
spending, WTI prices are likely to drift towards our 12-month 
target of USD 80 per barrel.

Democrats made solid gains across the Senate, House 
of Representatives and State Governors 
Results reported as at 7:45PM Singapore Time

Source: The Wall Street Journal, Standard Chartered

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 5 November 2026

Major implications of Democrat control of 
Congress   
1) Fiscal expansionary policies may slow. Democrats are 
now in position to block any new tax cuts or spending plans 
from the Trump administration. At the same time, any fiscal 
policies proposed by Democrats may also face Trump’s veto. 
Hence the fiscal trajectory of the US will likely remain stuck 
over the next two years. As we get into the second half of 
2027, the US debt ceiling will once again loom, and gridlock 
could potentially bring back government shutdowns. 
However, as they have always done in the past, compromise 
will ultimately prevail, in our opinion.  
2) Trump remains in control of foreign policy. With the 
midterms now out of the way, could Trump decide to re-start 
combat operations with Iran? We believe there is a risk that 
US and Israel – now that both countries’ have concluded 
important elections – will once again be tempted to restart 
combat operations to force an end to the conflict. However, 
the duration of any renewed hostilities may be limited by 
Congress next year as the Democrats will likely withhold 
funding for further military operations. In the near-term, oil 
prices may experience renewed volatility before stabilising at 
a lower level next year. The introduction of new tariffs on 
trading partners may prove harder with Democrat oversight. 
3) AI data centre moratoriums could grow, though model 
development regulatory changes would be harder to 
pass. While the headlines have focused on the House and 
Senate results, one important development has also 
occurred at the gubernatorial level. Notably, Democrats have 
won the governor races in Texas and Georgia, two big data 
centre states. As data centre build out permits are largely 
decided at the local state level, this will have important 
ramifications for build out plans next year. However, any 
Democratic proposals to regulate AI model developments 
would need to be signed off by President Trump, who 
continues to wield veto power on any such proposals.   
Asset class strategy 
Equities: Remain invested and continue to diversify.  
While investors initially overreacted, President Trump’s 
relatively conciliatory response has provided some welcome 
relief, helping markets recover strongly from their pre-market 
lows. Hence, we recommend investors avoid 
overreacting, remain invested and continue to diversify 
their equity exposure across sectors and regions.  
A few themes stand out from the early pre-market reaction to 
the mid-term elections. On the positive side, defensive stocks 
continue to fare well, while themes favoured by Democrats 
are also outperforming. Clean energy is a notable 
beneficiary, with solar stocks among the biggest winners

Policy uncertainty and a change in the innovation cycle 
is consistent with our view of a mid-cycle transition, 
where equity returns moderate but remain positive

today. Affordability-related themes are also rallying, including 
companies that could benefit from potential tariff rollbacks. 
Medicaid-related stocks are also moving higher on 
expectations that potential policy changes could provide a 
tailwind for the healthcare sector. 
On the negative side, semiconductor weakness continues 
amid concerns around potential data centre moratoriums. 
Our AI capex estimates remain conservative relative to 
consensus, but with a potential reset in expectations around 
data centre spending, investors should remain selective. 
After downgrading semiconductors last month, we have 
maintained a conservative stance on the industry, with a 
lower recommended allocation compared to September. 
While we believe the mid-term election outcome is not a 
complete surprise to markets, particularly given pre-election 
polling and the recent weakness in both the S&P 500 and 
Nasdaq, the overnight correction highlights how complacent 
investors had become, with the VIX initially surging more than 
10% in pre-market trading, although down from intra-day 
highs after President Trump’s comments. 
As we have highlighted in our recent notes on the transition 
to mid-cycle, this phase is typically characterised by greater 
political headwinds. The mid-term election outcome 
reinforces the trend. The experience of 2015-2017, 
highlighted in the second table, offers a useful historical 
parallel. Despite the knee-jerk reaction, investors should not 
overreact based on one day’s moves. While we had trimmed 
equities to a core allocation last week, we continue to expect 
equities to deliver mid-to-high single-digit returns per annum 
during the mid-cycle, even amid political headwinds, 
supported by steady underlying earnings growth in both 2027 
and 2028. 
So, what should investors do to navigate the next few months 
before the newly elected representatives are sworn in and 
take control of the House and Senate? As we have been 
highlighting in recent notes, equity markets should still deliver 
positive returns, albeit at a more moderate pace. Hence, we 
recommend staying invested but diversification remains key 
to navigating the next phase of the cycle.  
FX: DXY near-term support at 100.7, 12-month target at 
98.5. A Democratic sweep potentially removes the legislative 
runway for further tariff escalation and for fiscal expansion, 
the two structural pillars of US exceptionalism that have 
anchored dollar demand in 2026. Reduced fiscal impulse 
compresses the growth and yield differential that has kept the 
US dollar well-bid all year. Additionally, potential Democratic 
restrictions on AI and data centre investment could weigh on 
US equity outperformance, which is likely to act as a 
secondary dollar-negative channel.  
However, despite historical readings supporting a near-term 
DXY headwind from a Democratic sweep, the magnitude will

WS Global CIO Office | 5 November 2026

likely be modest and short-lived unless compounded by a 
weaker than expected non-farm payrolls report on Friday. We 
see the Fed narrative, not the election, as the dominant 
medium-term driver. Near-term we see USD index (DXY) 
support at 100.7, and retain our 12-month target at 98.5.  
Fixed Income: Still attractive all-in yields; prefer 
investment grade corporates at three-to-seven year 
tenors to earn income and manage interest rate risk. The 
US Treasury bond market reaction appears to reflect a 
moderation in expectations of future economic growth after 
the election results. Yields at the intermediate end of the 
curve dropped the most, while the 30-year US yield 
moderated more modestly. We continue to expect two more 
Fed rate hikes between now and H1 2027 as still sticky 
inflation and the robust economy support further tightening.  
In the past few weeks, the US Treasury yield curve has 
broadly shifted downwards, after our opportunistic call to go 
long 10-year Treasuries at the end of September. We are of 
the view the ultra-long end downshift in yields is overdone 
(given the still challenging US fiscal trajectory) and would 
recommend investors who had earlier taken long duration 
exposures at the 20 to 30-year parts of the curve reduce their 
positions now. We continue to believe the 3 to 7-year part of 
the curve offers the most optimal balance of income and 
interest rate risk, particularly via investment corporate bonds. 
Within corporate credit, we expect a potentially slower pace 
of AI data centre buildouts could marginally improve market 
technicals, as hyperscaler and tech sector bond issuance 
may slow (and reduce “crowding out” of other bond 
issuances). Conversely, from a credit perspective, we believe 
this dynamic may also make the data centre focused deals 
less viable, and would be cautious on some of this sector’s 
lower quality names. Meanwhile, less AI capex dependent 
issuers continue to offer solid credit profiles amidst the still 
strong economy. High yield corporate spreads have widened 
in recent weeks but dispersion within the complex is wide, 
with BBs and Bs credit spreads staying relatively resilient, 
while CCCs have widened to distressed levels. For income-
focused investors, we would recommend staying within the 
crossover range of between BBB-BB.  
Commodities: As noted in the opening section, we believe 
there is a higher near-term risk of a restart of combat 
operations in Iran given the conclusion of the midterm 
elections. Given that, we believe there is a risk that oil 
prices could see a short-term spike, despite recent 
diplomatic overtures from Iran. However, for 2027 we 
continue to expect oil to stabilise at lower levels, as we 
think the Mideast situation should de-escalate given 
Democratic oversight of the defense budget, and the likely re-
configuration of oil supply chains. On the margin, we are also 
closely monitoring regulatory developments in the US shale

We expect oil prices to drift towards USD 80 a barrel as 
oil supply chains adapt, and Congress restrains the US 
defense budget

On gold, we view the recent change in control in the 
Congress as only having a marginal impact and thus 
stay Overweight. USD strength is a headwind (which is 
more dependent on Fed policy), but gold’s sensitivity to real 
yields has eased amid continued central bank buying, led by 
China, and resilient investor demand.  
— Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO, Fixed Income & FX

## Document 338: CTCP Vàng Bạc Đá Quý Phú Nhuận - Cập nhật ĐHCĐ bất thường 21_10_2026 (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Vàng Bạc Đá Quý Phú Nhuận -  Cập nhật ĐHCĐ bất thường 21_10_2026 (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025.

Biểu đồ 1. Kết quả kinh doanh theo quý  Biểu đồ 2. Diễn biến doanh thu thuần/ngày và doanh thu bán 
lẻ/ngày theo quý

### Page 2

2

F I R S T  T O  M A R K E T 
 — PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ.

Phương án bổ sung vốn và tái cơ cấu hoạt động kinh doanh 
— Đại hội thông qua phương án chào bán tối đa 550 triệu cổ phiếu. Nguồn vốn dự kiến 
phân bổ 60% cho kế hoạch kinh doanh gắn với điều chỉnh chính sách thu đổi, 20% bổ 
sung vốn lưu động và phần còn lại bổ sung vốn lưu động và/hoặc trả vay ngắn hạn. 
Nhu cầu vốn Q4/2026 và Q1/2027 lần lượt khoảng 3,100 tỷ VND và 2,800 tỷ VND. Giá và 
khối lượng phát hành thực tế chưa được xác định tại đại hội.  
— Doanh nghiệp dự kiến đóng 25–30 cửa hàng và mở khoảng 10 cửa hàng (có chọn lọc) 
trong năm 2026, tương ứng giảm ròng 15–20 cửa hàng; đồng thời đặt mục tiêu giảm 
20–30% chi phí nhân sự. Cổ tức tiền mặt năm 2026 được điều chỉnh về 0%; cổ tức 2025 
về 10% mệnh giá và đã chi trả. 
— 100% mẫu kim cương được AIGS và Vinacontrol kiểm định độc lập là kim cương thiên 
nhiên, phù hợp thông số 4C. PNJ dự kiến trang bị máy kiểm định tại các cửa hàng trọng 
điểm để khách hàng kiểm tra trực tiếp.

Đánh giá của KBSV 
— KBSV cho rằng huy động vốn từ phát hành riêng lẻ là phương án phù hợp ở thời điểm 
hiện tại trong bối cảnh lãi suất tăng cao, giúp PNJ cân đối thanh khoản và duy trì hoạt 
động liên tục. Tuy nhiên, với khối lượng phát hành lớn và chưa có phương án chi tiết, 
doanh nghiệp sẽ cần thêm thời gian để hoàn thành phương án phát hành và cân đối 
giữa lợi ích của cổ đông và doanh nghiệp.  
— Nhu cầu yếu tại Trung Quốc và cạnh tranh từ kim cương nuôi cấy giá thấp tiếp tục gây 
áp lực lên giá kim cương tự nhiên, khiến PNJ gặp khó khăn trong việc tiêu thụ tồn kho 
nhóm kim cương  và gia tăng tổn thất khi mua lại theo chính sách cũ. Thực tế, tỷ trọng 
kim cương và trang sức kim cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% cuối Q2/2026 lên 
52% cuối Q3/2026, với giá trị ước khoảng 9,000 tỷ VND. KBSV cho rằng cần theo dõi (1) 
nhu cầu thị trường trong giai đoạn cuối năm; (2) tốc độ giảm tồn kho và dòng tiền thu 
về từ bán hàng; và (3) giá trị khách hàng bán lại. Diễn biến các yếu tố này sẽ quyết định 
khả năng thu hồi vốn, mức độ phục hồi biên lợi nhuận và nhu cầu trích lập bổ sung 
trong quá trình xử lý. 
— Ước tính bán niên sử dụng tỷ lệ tổn thất 20.3% đối với hàng bán trong giai đoạn 2020–
2026 dự kiến được thu mua lại, thấp hơn mức 28.9% trên các giao dịch mua lại thực tế 
từ 01/07–03/09/2026. Vì vậy, bên cạnh giả định thu đổi 80%, mức tổn thất khi xử lý 
hàng mua lại là yếu tố quan trọng cần theo dõi. Việc chuyển hạch toán dự phòng từ 
SG&A sang giá vốn tại kỳ soát xét bán niên cũng cho thấy áp lực biên lợi nhuận bị co 
hẹp trong quá trình tái cấu trúc về mặt kế toán.

## Document 358: First Impression_ RBNZ survey of inflation expectations, December quarter 2026 (2026-11-17)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impression_ RBNZ survey of inflation expectations, December quarter 2026 (2026-11-17).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#inflation #newzealand
First Impression: RBNZ survey of inflation expectations,
December quarter 2026
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
13:50 November 17 2026 Share
Inflation expectations remained contained in the RBNZ’s latest survey. High oil prices and
elevated near-term inflation don’t appear to have boosted medium-term inflation
expectations.
RBNZ inflation expectations survey

### Page 2

1 year ahead: 2.92% (Prev: 2.60%, up 32bps)
2 years ahead: 2.36% (Prev: 2.34%, up 2bps)
5 years ahead: 2.17% (Prev: 2.31%, down 14bps)
10 years ahead: 2.13% (Prev: 2.20%, down 7bp)

There was only limited movement in the RBNZ’s latest survey of inflation expectations. 
Importantly for the RBNZ’s policy deliberations ahead of their December policy meeting, the detail of
today’s update points to inflation expectations remaining well contained despite the current oil-related
lift in inflation.

Looking at the details of the December inflation expectations update:
Expectations for inflation 1 year ahead did rise to 2.92% (vs 2.60% last quarter). That’s consistent with
the sharp rise in fuel prices in recent months, which we expect will push overall inflation up to 4.3% by
the end of this year. 
However, expectations for inflation at longer horizons were much more stable. Notably, the closely
watched measure of inflation in two years’ time nudged just slightly higher, rising to 2.36% (vs 2.34%
previously). 
Similarly, expectations for inflation at long horizons (5 and 10 years ahead) actually slipped back this
quarter and now sit just a little above 2%.

Implications for the RBNZ
Today’s result will be welcome news for the RBNZ. With oil prices pushing inflation to high levels, there
was a risk that expectations for inflation would take a step higher, adding to the pressure on prices over
the year ahead. Such risks had been alluded to in a recent speech by the RBNZ’s Chief Economist.
Nevertheless, the RBNZ is still looking at an elevated near-term inflation outlook. Importantly, the
strength in inflation isn’t just due to high oil prices. Core inflation has remained at firm levels for an
extended period. 
Consistent with those firm underlying inflation pressures, we continue to expect an OCR hike at the
RBNZ’s upcoming 9 December policy meeting. 
We’ll discuss our thoughts in more detail in our latest Economic Overview, out tomorrow.

Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist

## Document 366: Germany’s hidden advantage in the AI race

Source PDF: all report pdf/Germany’s hidden advantage in the AI race.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 4

### Page 1

Berlin may be Germany’s innovation powerhouse, but across
Europe, the country is still playing catch-up. When it comes to
AI, however, it could have much more up its sleeve than its
overall innovation performance suggests

Franziska Biehl and Carsten Brzeski
Once we turn from traditional innovation to artiﬁcial intelligence, Germany
suddenly looks much more competitive
Germany’s innovation problem runs deep…
Germany’s innovation landscape remains highly fragmented. Berlin
tops our national Innovation Index for the 12th consecutive year,
followed by Hamburg and Baden-Württemberg, while Thuringia,
Mecklenburg-Western Pomerania and Saxony-Anhalt remain at the
bottom, held back by demographic pressures, skills shortages and
weak business creation.
These regional diﬀerences reﬂect the structural challenges holding
back innovation capacity across the country. Consequently,
Germany only ranks 16th out of the 21 eurozone countries in our
ING Germany Innovation Index for the eurozone, leaving one of
